# Source menus compared with the unified client

The unified client exposes useful common controls, but it does not yet expose the source menu feature union. The largest gaps are prelaunch server configuration, the complete binding editor, player identity, Q3 progression/team/bot menus, and the deeper LMCTF administrative menus. Existing backend flags do not count as completed menu controls.

## Scope and evidence

Compared the actual TypeScript menus in quake-1-re-ts at `6bc6a8bf29b66981e3b6ce7251ebbc6413120270`, quake-2-re-ts at `0d73750cbe5683c7411934d0a5d4eb5acd4da676`, and quake-3-ts at `8453c49824eb7a5ed5aee452f74e19336965d1f8`. The common-client baseline is `392b3e37293bb8c9720255c6c958b28be50b482a`. Source repositories and retail content were read only. No application, network, audio or global tests were run for this document.

Definitions were followed into callbacks, cvar writes, commands, menu scripts, source-event consumers and shared settings owners. The supporting extraction retains exact file/line references and hashes. Team Arena definitions were read from the installed local missionpack PK3 overlay, because its donor loads external `.menu` files; inspecting only its generic parser would omit actual controls. The `ui/...menu:line` citations below identify those exact extracted members. The source handlers are [menu scripts](../../quake-3-ts/src/ui/team-arena/menu-scripts.ts#L280), [owner keys](../../quake-3-ts/src/ui/team-arena/owner-keys.ts#L195), [settings updates](../../quake-3-ts/src/ui/team-arena/settings.ts#L60), and [menu loading](../../quake-3-ts/src/ui/team-arena/menu-loader.ts#L126).

This is a control checklist, not an engine completion audit. Each numbered row is one enumerated source control/action or meaningful variant. Dynamic list choices such as installed maps are one list control rather than one requirement per map. Identical Team Arena controls repeated in startup/in-game assets are grouped with all source locations; decoration is excluded. Different games' variants remain separate so their semantics are not silently erased. These are overlapping donor-control records, not 1123 independent engine features. The counts below apply only to these 1123 rows. They do not modify completion-status.json or count files read as completed features.

| Checklist state | Rows | Meaning |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Present (source verified) | 129 | A reachable equivalent control and actual shared write/action owner were traced in source. This is not a claim of a new runtime acceptance test. |
| Open | 994 | Missing, partial, backend-only, or limited by a broken/inert source control. |
| Total | 1123 | Only the individually numbered rows below. |

Open-unexposed means the source control is absent from the common menu. It makes no claim that an equivalent backend works; explicit owner evidence or an unresolved backend boundary is stated per row. Where only a same-named backend declaration is located, the row says so rather than claiming its application route works. Open-partial means a usable portion exists but source scope, choices or lifecycle differs. Open-missing means the required application workflow/owner is absent. Open-source-limited records a donor control whose advertised behavior is itself missing or incorrect; it remains unresolved rather than becoming a fake working toggle.

## In-flight changes

The video owner is modifying a common Display category at startup and in-game: live gamma/reset, real display-mode enumeration, custom width/height, borderless fullscreen and GL vsync through common renderer/window/settings owners. Monster source filtering, an unmatched-class fallback and a complete native-slot roster are also in-flight changes and are excluded from the baseline roster description. Those changes were not accepted when this baseline was captured and remain Open-partial here. They touch src/app/bootstrap/renderer.ts, src/platform/sdl.ts, src/ui/settings/services.ts, src/app/bootstrap/ui.ts, src/app/bootstrap/startup.ts, src/app/bootstrap/startup-menu.ts and existing image-settings/application refresh paths. Recheck the final accepted artifact before changing these verdicts.

| Options > Video | Baseline window size; replacement-image policy; image-format ordering; skin/sprite/wall/picture/sky replacement toggles; Q1 enhanced models; load/draw Q2 enhanced models; model range | Existing cvars have runtime refresh owners. [image/model controls](../src/ui/settings/images.ts#L6) |
| Options > Audio | Output device; effects volume; music volume | ApplicationAudio applies changes. [audio binding](../src/app/bootstrap/ui.ts#L123) |
| Options > Accessibility | HUD size; text size; menu size; crosshair size; high contrast; reduce HUD flashes; captions; crosshair visibility | Captions toggle exists, but no complete event producer; not proof of caption workflow. [preferences](../src/ui/settings/index.ts#L199) |
| Options > Language | Mounted per-seat Q2 rerelease language choice and reload | Limited to caller-provided language binding; no uniform translated menu chrome. [binding](../src/app/bootstrap/rerelease-presentation.ts#L45) |
| Options > Network > Server settings | Available source-owned settings; requested/effective/default/timing; Apply; Use default; paging | Q2/Q3 owners only, after launch; Q1 yields no collection. [selection](../src/settings/server/selection.ts#L8) |
| Server profiles | Profile name; save desired settings; load/apply; validation feedback | Selected admitted server settings only, not arbitrary client/mod config. [profiles](../src/ui/settings/server.ts#L97) |
| Startup browser | Game family; address; Query; Find LAN; favorite toggle; text filter; sorting; hide empty/full; paging; favorites-only; Connect | Real shared browser owner. No master configuration, complete details, passwords or per-wire protocol selector. [browser](../src/app/bootstrap/startup-menu.ts#L180) |
| Startup Load Game | Saved rows; availability reason; older pages; refresh; selected load | No paired in-game Save menu. [load](../src/app/bootstrap/startup-menu.ts#L151) |
| Source-event match menus | Q2 CTF join/chase/credits/match/admin events and LMCTF basic join/vote events | [Q2MatchUi](../src/app/bootstrap/q2-match-ui.ts#L60), not the full donor referee tree. |
| Remote play | Same ApplicationSeatUi for the remote local seat, without host settings argument | [remote construction](../src/app/bootstrap/remote-application.ts#L504). Do not imply remote hosts can be configured with local server settings. |

## Unique capability groups

This summary groups overlapping donor variants into 14 user workflows. A group is not declared complete because some controls are present. Detailed rows retain the distinct source values and restrictions; navigation duplicates and per-game variants do not inflate this summary into independent features.

| Shared capability | Present at the accepted baseline (source verified) | Remaining controls/workflow |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start and select content | Campaign/map, movement, character, equipment, difficulty and seats | Independent native gamecode/data rulesets; complete mode eligibility; arbitrary add-on admission |
| Save and restore play | Startup common-save browser and load dispatch | In-game Save/overwrite/slot management; donor save-format qualification |
| Display and rendering | CPU/GL choice, fixed sizes, startup gamma, image/model replacement policies | Live/native/custom modes/fullscreen work in flight; render scale, viewport, quality, lighting/shadow/texture policies |
| Audio and music | Output device, effects/music volume, environment/Doppler | Quality/rate/format policy and explicit playback enable |
| Bind actions | Seven captured actions through shared binding owner | Full movement/combat/inventory/chat/team union; clear, secondary and defaults |
| Mouse, controller and seats | Sampled mouse/stick tuning, vibration, gyro calibration, seat count | Device assignment/disable chooser, disconnected-device UX, source-specific input policies |
| Player identity | Startup character source/model selection | Per-seat name, skin, color, handicap, handedness and visual preview |
| Host match | Basic mode/rules plus admitted Q2/Q3 settings after launch | Complete prelaunch limits, capacity/access/protocol, Q1 rules, bots/teams, expansion and mod controls |
| Map rotation | Starting map selector | Ordered list, add/remove/reorder/shuffle, named save/load and mode-filtered search |
| Browse and connect | Remote address/port, Connect dispatch, LAN, favorites, text/basic filters and bidirectional sorts | Masters, passwords, detailed rules/players, remaining filters and wire choices |
| In-game management | Resume, Console, End game; source-event Q2 match subset | Explicit leave/quit/restart, full Q3 teams/bots/orders and LMCTF referee/radio/vote trees |
| Progression and media | General campaign/map launch | Q3 tier/skill/rank/medal/reset/unlock/postgame workflow; demos/movies/mod library |
| Configuration and downloads | Admitted host setting profiles and per-setting defaults | Client profiles/import/export/reset, download policy, licenses and source-specific config controls |
| Accessibility and navigation | UI/HUD/text/crosshair size, visibility, contrast/flash preferences, mounted Q2 language selection | Crosshair shapes, full captions production, translated shared chrome, help/credits and consistent startup/in-game reachability |

## Donor limits that should not become misleading controls

- Q1 serial/modem and IPX entries are unreachable in the donor, and QW single-player/multiplayer screens are explanatory placeholders. They are not counted as working source menu options. See [Q1 network dispatch](../../quake-1-re-ts/src/client/menu.ts#L1893) and [QW placeholders](../../quake-1-re-ts/src/qw/client/menu.ts#L1120).
- Q2 LMCTF weapon-balance/voice bits are documented as compiled out, autoobserve as stored-only, random-map choice as a readout, and certain rerelease options as having no reader. These are recorded in their individual server rows. A settings union should expose real capability, not reproduce inert storage.
- Team Arena Sync Every Frame incorrectly binds `weapon 5` in both options assets. Base Q3 correctly writes `r_finish`. This difference is recorded rather than concealed behind the label.
- Q3 Voodoo selection is intentionally unavailable in the donor SDL backend. PunkBuster settings do not establish an installed anti-cheat implementation. Neither justifies an enabled unsupported selector in the common UI.
- Static help text, logos, credits text, frames, score readouts and duplicate button artwork are not separate controls. Their actual navigation, selection and playback actions are listed. HUD-only Team Arena assets have no configuration actions.

## Improvements after parity

These recommendations are separate from the counted factual checklist. They describe a better shared configuration experience, not extra completed features.

1. Keep one Settings entry at startup and during play, with the same category names and controls. Use Video, Audio, Controls, Players, Gameplay, Accessibility and Network. Show host controls only to the host and keep their drafts available before launch. The current separate startup and in-game builders expose different subsets.
2. Make Host Game a complete workflow: select map/content and real mode, configure capacity and teams/bots, adjust limits/rules, then Play. Keep weapons, movement, character and world independently selectable. Label Q3 tournament/CTF/One Flag/Overload/Harvester explicitly; do not force players to launch first and change g_gametype for the next map.
3. Turn the flat seven-row server pages and second detail screen into coherent groups with inline controls: Match, Teams, Items, Weapons, Players, Access, Map rotation and Advanced. Retain source descriptions, defaults, desired/effective values and apply timing. A friendly name should accompany source-specific semantics rather than hide them.
4. Offer native display modes with readable dimensions/aspect/refresh, custom dimensions, fullscreen/windowed and a safe display-change confirmation. Separate window size from render scale and UI/HUD/console scale. Gamma, volume and simple presentation changes should apply live; renderer recreation should happen only when required. The in-flight video work is the first part of this workflow.
5. Make the binding editor complete and searchable by action. Group movement, combat, inventory, communication and team orders. Support primary/secondary assignments, clear, conflict resolution, source-specific weapon names, controller button capture and per-category reset. Keep the existing device-family capture owner.
6. Add Players & Devices before launch: each seat has a name/model/skin/color, assigned controller or keyboard/mouse, disconnected-device state, vibration and a small live input preview. Persist stable identities, not transient SDL instance numbers. Gyro calibration belongs beside that selected device.
7. Provide named client and host profiles with separate reset-current-category, reset-current-profile and import/export actions. Explain what will change before reset. Preserve the common ConfigStore rather than create separate per-game stores. Gameplay saves and configuration profiles should remain distinct user concepts.
8. Improve the browser with selectable LAN/master/favorites sources, editable direct hosts and master endpoints, server/player detail, password prompt, native protocol choice when needed, visible refreshing/cancel/error state, and sort columns with direction. Reuse the current persistent hostname/favorite owner.
9. Respect dependencies visibly: shadow quality disabled without shadows; gyro controls disabled with a specific capability reason; unavailable source assets explained; team options shown only for team modes; dedicated/public/password settings tied to actual host support. Do not offer a setting merely because a cvar can store its value.
10. Use source defaults for rules and sensible machine defaults for devices/display. Describe effects in plain terms, including live/next-map/restart timing and units. Keep details available on focus for keyboard and controller as well as hover for mouse.
11. Keep all controls reachable by mouse, keyboard and controller with consistent Back/Cancel/Apply semantics and scroll/paging. Preserve focus after reload and avoid silently moving the selected item. Every newly exposed option should be exercised through its actual menu action and checked in the resulting owner.
12. Add discoverable Save/Load, Leave session, Restart match, player/team/bot management, demos/movies and progression screens only as their real shared workflows are connected. Opening an empty or disabled submenu does not close a checklist row.

## Verification and limits

The extraction enumerated source control definitions and followed menu callbacks. The document generator checked row uniqueness, totals and references. No new runtime test, visual parity, physical controller, wire interoperability or full source-game feature claim is made here. The supporting exact source snapshot and SHA256 manifest live under `/tmp/quake-menu-comparison/frozen-revision3`. Production code, old completion counters and historical status documents were not edited.
